# Current calendar, independent of diagnostic refresh.
sed_live_month_key = { value = year multiply = 12 add = month }
# Country scope. Fixed expenditure is gross fixed income minus its net balance.
sed_fixed_expenses_value = { value = fixed_income subtract = net_fixed_income min = 0 }
# State scope; demographic proxy rather than a worker count from the engine.
sed_state_labor_pool = {
 value = state_population min = 0
 multiply = { value = 0.25 add = modifier:state_working_adult_ratio_add min = 0.05 max = 1 }
 multiply = {
  value = state_unemployment_rate min = 0 max = 1
  add = { value = "pop_type_percent_state(pop_type:peasants)" min = 0 max = 1 multiply = 0.5 }
  max = 1
 }
}

sed_state_labor_available = {
 value = sed_state_labor_pool subtract = sed_state_labor_committed min = 0
}
sed_input_railway_coal = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { b:building_railway = { has_active_production_method = pm_early_trains } } add = 2 }

 if = { limit = { b:building_railway = { has_active_production_method = pm_steam_trains_principle_transport_3 } } add = 6 }
 min = 0
}

 }

sed_input_country_headroom_coal = {
 value = 0
 market ?= { mg:coal ?= { add = { value = market_goods_sell_orders min = 0 multiply = 2 subtract = { value = market_goods_buy_orders min = 0 } } } }
 subtract = { value = 0 every_scope_state = { limit = { } add = sed_input_pending_coal } }
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_pm_inputs_month var:sed_pm_inputs_month = sed_live_month_key
  has_variable = sed_pm_reserved_coal
 } subtract = var:sed_pm_reserved_coal }
}
sed_input_headroom_coal = { value = owner.var:sed_input_market_margin_coal }

 market ?= { mg:oil ?= { add = { value = market_goods_sell_orders min = 0 multiply = 2 subtract = { value = market_goods_buy_orders min = 0 } } } }
 subtract = { value = 0 every_scope_state = { limit = { } add = sed_input_pending_oil } }
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_pm_inputs_month var:sed_pm_inputs_month = sed_live_month_key
  has_variable = sed_pm_reserved_oil
 } subtract = var:sed_pm_reserved_oil }
}
sed_input_headroom_oil = { value = owner.var:sed_input_market_margin_oil }
sed_input_pending_rubber = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { has_building = building_tooling_workshop }
  add = { value = sed_input_tooling_workshop_rubber
   multiply = { value = b:building_tooling_workshop.level_after_queued_constructions
    subtract = { value = b:building_tooling_workshop.level multiply = { value = b:building_tooling_workshop.occupancy min = 0 max = 1 } }
    min = 0
   }
   multiply = 1.25
  }
 }
 if = { limit = { has_building = building_textile_mill }
  add = { value = sed_input_textile_mill_rubber
   multiply = { value = b:building_textile_mill.level_after_queued_constructions
    subtract = { value = b:building_textile_mill.level multiply = { value = b:building_textile_mill.occupancy min = 0 max = 1 } }
    min = 0
   }
   multiply = 1.25
  }
 }
 if = { limit = {
  NOT = { has_building = building_tooling_workshop }
  has_variable = sed_tools_request_pending
  var:sed_tools_intent_level = 0
var:sed_tools_intent_variant = 1
 } add = { value = sed_founding_input_tooling_workshop_rubber multiply = 1.25 } }
 if = { limit = {
  NOT = { has_building = building_textile_mill }
  has_variable = sed_clothes_request_pending
  var:sed_clothes_intent_level = 0
var:sed_clothes_intent_variant = 1
 } add = { value = sed_founding_input_textile_mill_rubber multiply = 1.25 } }
}

  has_variable = sed_coal_request_pending
  var:sed_coal_intent_level = 0

 } add = { value = sed_founding_input_coal_mine_transportation multiply = 1.25 } }

  NOT = { has_building = building_cotton_plantation }
  has_variable = sed_fabric_request_pending

sed_input_local_headroom_transportation = {
 value = 0
 sg:transportation ?= { add = { value = state_goods_production min = 0 multiply = 2 subtract = { value = state_goods_consumption min = 0 } } }
 subtract = sed_input_pending_transportation
 if = { limit = { owner = { has_variable = sed_pm_inputs_month var:sed_pm_inputs_month = sed_live_month_key }
  has_variable = sed_pm_reserved_transportation
 } subtract = var:sed_pm_reserved_transportation }
}

 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sed_input_local_margin_transportation } } add = sed_input_local_headroom_transportation }
}
sed_input_pending_wood = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { has_building = building_railway }
  add = { value = sed_input_railway_wood
   multiply = { value = b:building_railway.level_after_queued_constructions
    subtract = { value = b:building_railway.level multiply = { value = b:building_railway.occupancy min = 0 max = 1 } }
    min = 0
   }
   multiply = 1.25
  }
 }
 if = { limit = { has_building = building_construction_sector }
  add = { value = sed_input_construction_sector_wood
   multiply = { value = b:building_construction_sector.level_after_queued_constructions
    subtract = { value = b:building_construction_sector.level multiply = { value = b:building_construction_sector.occupancy min = 0 max = 1 } }

    subtract = { value = b:building_paper_mill.level multiply = { value = b:building_paper_mill.occupancy min = 0 max = 1 } }

   }

  }

 value = 0

 subtract = { value = 0 every_scope_state = { limit = { } add = sed_input_pending_wood } }
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_pm_inputs_month var:sed_pm_inputs_month = sed_live_month_key
  has_variable = sed_pm_reserved_wood
 } subtract = var:sed_pm_reserved_wood }
}

sed_investment_market_price_wood_lower = {
 value = 15
 market ?= { mg:wood ?= {
 if = { limit = { market_goods_pricier > 0 } add = { value = market_goods_pricier multiply = 20 } }
 if = { limit = { market_goods_cheaper > 0 } subtract = { value = market_goods_cheaper multiply = 20 } }
 } }
 min = 0
 max = 40
}
sed_investment_observed_price_wood_lower = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { sg:wood ?= { state_goods_production >= 0 } }
  add = 15
  sg:wood ?= {
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper > 0 } subtract = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.1 } add = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.1 } subtract = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.2 } add = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.2 } subtract = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.3 } add = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.3 } subtract = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.4 } add = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.4 } subtract = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.5 } add = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.5 } subtract = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.6 } add = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.6 } subtract = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.7 } add = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.7 } subtract = 2 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.75 } add = 1 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.75 } subtract = 1 }
  }
 }
 if = { limit = { NOT = { sg:wood ?= { state_goods_production >= 0 } } } add = owner.var:sed_investment_market_wood_lower }
 min = 0
 max = 40
}

 market ?= { mg:ammunition ?= {
 if = { limit = { market_goods_pricier > 0 } add = { value = market_goods_pricier multiply = 50 } }
 if = { limit = { market_goods_cheaper > 0 } subtract = { value = market_goods_cheaper multiply = 50 } }
 } }
 min = 0
 max = 100
}
sed_investment_observed_price_ammunition_upper = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { sg:ammunition ?= { state_goods_production >= 0 } }
  add = 62.5
  sg:ammunition ?= {
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier > 0 } add = 5 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.1 } add = 5 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.1 } subtract = 5 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.2 } add = 5 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.2 } subtract = 5 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.3 } add = 5 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.3 } subtract = 5 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.4 } add = 5 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.4 } subtract = 5 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.5 } add = 5 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.5 } subtract = 5 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.6 } add = 5 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.6 } subtract = 5 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.7 } add = 5 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.7 } subtract = 5 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_pricier >= 0.75 } add = 2.5 }
 if = { limit = { state_goods_cheaper >= 0.75 } subtract = 2.5 }
  }
 }
 if = { limit = { NOT = { sg:ammunition ?= { state_goods_production >= 0 } } } add = owner.var:sed_investment_market_ammunition_upper }
 min = 0
 max = 100
}
sed_investment_price_ammunition_upper = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_local_ammunition_upper } add = var:sed_investment_local_ammunition_upper }
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sed_investment_local_ammunition_upper } } add = sed_investment_observed_price_ammunition_upper }
}

sed_investment_logging_camp_output_hardwood = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { b:building_logging_camp = { has_active_production_method = pm_hardwood } } add = 10 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_logging_camp = { has_active_production_method = pm_increased_hardwood } } add = 20 }
 min = 0
}
sed_investment_logging_camp_output_wood = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { b:building_logging_camp = { has_active_production_method = pm_simple_forestry } } add = 30 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_logging_camp = { has_active_production_method = pm_saw_mills } } add = 60 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_logging_camp = { has_active_production_method = pm_electric_saw_mills } } add = 100 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_logging_camp = { has_active_production_method = pm_hardwood } } add = -25 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_logging_camp = { has_active_production_method = pm_increased_hardwood } } add = -40 }
 min = 0
}
sed_investment_logging_camp_margin = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_logging_camp } }

  add = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_hardwood_lower }
  add = { value = 30 multiply = sed_investment_price_wood_lower }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_coal_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_electricity_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_engines_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_oil_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_tools_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_transportation_upper }
 }
 if = { limit = { has_building = building_logging_camp }

  add = { value = sed_investment_logging_camp_output_hardwood multiply = sed_investment_price_hardwood_lower }
  add = { value = sed_investment_logging_camp_output_wood multiply = sed_investment_price_wood_lower }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_logging_camp_coal multiply = sed_investment_price_coal_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_logging_camp_electricity multiply = sed_investment_price_electricity_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_logging_camp_engines multiply = sed_investment_price_engines_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_logging_camp_oil multiply = sed_investment_price_oil_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_logging_camp_tools multiply = sed_investment_price_tools_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_logging_camp_transportation multiply = sed_investment_price_transportation_upper }
 }
}
sed_investment_logging_camp_output = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_logging_camp } } add = 30 }
 if = { limit = { has_building = building_logging_camp } add = sed_investment_logging_camp_output_wood }
}
sed_investment_logging_camp_return = {
 value = sed_investment_logging_camp_margin
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known_logging_camp var:sed_investment_wage_known_logging_camp = 1 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_logging_camp } }
   subtract = { value = 6000 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit_logging_camp }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_logging_camp }
   subtract = { value = sed_investment_logging_camp_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit_logging_camp }
  }
 }
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known_logging_camp var:sed_investment_wage_known_logging_camp = 1 } }
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known var:sed_investment_wage_known = 1 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_logging_camp } }
   subtract = { value = 6000 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_logging_camp }
   subtract = { value = sed_investment_logging_camp_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
 }
 }
}
sed_investment_logging_camp_return_national = {
 value = sed_investment_logging_camp_margin
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known var:sed_investment_wage_known = 1 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_logging_camp } }
   subtract = { value = 6000 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_logging_camp }
   subtract = { value = sed_investment_logging_camp_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
 }
}

sed_investment_iron_mine_margin = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_iron_mine } }

  add = { value = 20 multiply = sed_investment_price_iron_lower }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_coal_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_engines_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_explosives_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_oil_upper }
  subtract = { value = 5 multiply = sed_investment_price_tools_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_transportation_upper }
 }
 if = { limit = { has_building = building_iron_mine }

  add = { value = sed_investment_iron_mine_output_iron multiply = sed_investment_price_iron_lower }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_iron_mine_coal multiply = sed_investment_price_coal_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_iron_mine_engines multiply = sed_investment_price_engines_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_iron_mine_explosives multiply = sed_investment_price_explosives_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_iron_mine_oil multiply = sed_investment_price_oil_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_iron_mine_tools multiply = sed_investment_price_tools_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_iron_mine_transportation multiply = sed_investment_price_transportation_upper }
 }
}
sed_investment_iron_mine_output = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_iron_mine } } add = 20 }
 if = { limit = { has_building = building_iron_mine } add = sed_investment_iron_mine_output_iron }
}
sed_investment_iron_mine_return = {
 value = sed_investment_iron_mine_margin
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known_iron_mine var:sed_investment_wage_known_iron_mine = 1 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_iron_mine } }
   subtract = { value = 6000 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit_iron_mine }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_iron_mine }
   subtract = { value = sed_investment_iron_mine_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit_iron_mine }
  }
 }
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known_iron_mine var:sed_investment_wage_known_iron_mine = 1 } }
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known var:sed_investment_wage_known = 1 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_iron_mine } }
   subtract = { value = 6000 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_iron_mine }

 }

  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_munition_plant } }
   subtract = { value = 7500 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit_munition_plant }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_munition_plant }
   subtract = { value = sed_investment_munition_plant_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit_munition_plant }
  }
 }
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known_munition_plant var:sed_investment_wage_known_munition_plant = 1 } }
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known var:sed_investment_wage_known = 1 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_munition_plant } }
   subtract = { value = 7500 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_munition_plant }
   subtract = { value = sed_investment_munition_plant_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
 }
 }
}
sed_investment_munition_plant_return_national = {
 value = sed_investment_munition_plant_margin
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known var:sed_investment_wage_known = 1 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_munition_plant } }
   subtract = { value = 7500 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_munition_plant }
   subtract = { value = sed_investment_munition_plant_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
 }
}
sed_investment_ammunition_margin = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { sed_ammunition_munition_plant_investment_eligible = yes } add = sed_investment_munition_plant_margin }
}
sed_investment_ammunition_output = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { sed_ammunition_munition_plant_investment_eligible = yes } add = sed_investment_munition_plant_output }
}
sed_investment_ammunition_return = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { sed_ammunition_munition_plant_investment_eligible = yes } add = sed_investment_munition_plant_return }
}
sed_investment_paper_mill_output_paper = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { b:building_paper_mill = { has_active_production_method = pm_pulp_pressing } } add = 40 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_paper_mill = { has_active_production_method = pm_sulfite_pulping } } add = 70 }
 if = { limit = { b:building_paper_mill = { has_active_production_method = pm_bleached_paper } } add = 100 }
 min = 0
}
sed_investment_paper_mill_margin = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_paper_mill } }

  add = { value = 40 multiply = sed_investment_price_paper_lower }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_coal_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_dye_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_sulfur_upper }
  subtract = { value = 0 multiply = sed_investment_price_tools_upper }
  subtract = { value = 30 multiply = sed_investment_price_wood_upper }
 }
 if = { limit = { has_building = building_paper_mill }

  add = { value = sed_investment_paper_mill_output_paper multiply = sed_investment_price_paper_lower }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_paper_mill_coal multiply = sed_investment_price_coal_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_paper_mill_dye multiply = sed_investment_price_dye_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_paper_mill_sulfur multiply = sed_investment_price_sulfur_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_paper_mill_tools multiply = sed_investment_price_tools_upper }
  subtract = { value = sed_input_paper_mill_wood multiply = sed_investment_price_wood_upper }
 }
}
sed_investment_paper_mill_output = {
 value = 0
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_paper_mill } } add = 40 }
 if = { limit = { has_building = building_paper_mill } add = sed_investment_paper_mill_output_paper }
}
sed_investment_paper_mill_return = {
 value = sed_investment_paper_mill_margin
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known_paper_mill var:sed_investment_wage_known_paper_mill = 1 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_paper_mill } }
   subtract = { value = 6000 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit_paper_mill }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_paper_mill }
   subtract = { value = sed_investment_paper_mill_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit_paper_mill }
  }
 }
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known_paper_mill var:sed_investment_wage_known_paper_mill = 1 } }
 if = { limit = { has_variable = sed_investment_wage_known var:sed_investment_wage_known = 1 }
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_building = building_paper_mill } }
   subtract = { value = 6000 multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
  if = { limit = { has_building = building_paper_mill }
   subtract = { value = sed_investment_paper_mill_weighted_jobs multiply = var:sed_investment_wage_unit }
  }
 }
 }
}
